Forecast curriculum: learn 3 days, forecast, keep score, refit on 6, 12, 24 ... days.
Accuracy = right / (right + wrong); unchanged bars and abstentions are not scored.
Significance is Bonferroni-corrected over 2303 model-track tests.

Tradability: the accuracy a call needs to pay its costs, p* = 1/2 + cost / (2 mean |move|),
at the history's average cost and at today's (STT 5 bp since 2026-04-01)
